## ALIVO editorial illustrations

The following ALIVO editorial illustrations were generated specifically for ALIVO with OpenAI ImageGen on 2026-11-03. They are not instructional demonstrations. The source link in the manifest points to the publicly accessible ImageGen service terms; it is not a Creative Commons license for these works. Rights and project usage are managed by ALIVO. The bundled files are scaled by the interface. Some illustrations were recolored and given ALIVO clothing wordmarks with OpenAI ImageGen.
